# Original-game parity scorecard

Whole-game accuracy: **not yet measurable**. This is a version-1 inventory of selected
behavior obligations, not a representative or complete sample of the entire game.
Feature coverage and passing rewrite tests do not establish original-game parity.

Active inventory: **211 obligations**; **169 matched**, **24 known differences**, **18 untested/unresolved**.
Matched acceptance: 23 exact, 19 numerical, 127 structural.
Separately tracked: 1 deferred; 0 outside original-parity scope.

A match is limited to its stated scenario, evidence and tolerance. Static source agreement
is labeled separately from original-runtime agreement. Missing evidence downgrades a
recorded conclusion to untested on the current machine. Changed pinned evidence also requires
review before its conclusion is counted. Unknowns are never counted as matches.

Exact matches require equality of the stated outputs; numerical matches meet a declared
error bound; structural matches establish a traced control/data relationship without
a paired-output equality result. None alone proves an entire subsystem accurate.

## Obligation inventory

| Area | Static matches | Runtime matches | Known differences | Untested | Deferred/excluded |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Audio | 16 | 3 | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| Gameplay and animation | 26 | 27 | 16 | 3 | 0 |
| Modes, progression and saves | 17 | 7 | 2 | 1 | 1 |
| Presentation, camera and menus | 15 | 58 | 6 | 13 | 0 |

Exact equality and tolerance-based agreement are different acceptance criteria.
A zero exact-match count can coexist with very small numerical errors; read the bounds.

- **audio.action_selector_coverage**: Action header keys matching original unpatched MIPS PLAY lookup
  - Acceptance: Exact integer PLAY selector equality.
  - Limits: Executed successfully 2026-10-13; all 3969 combined keys verified. This measures bounded selector construction, not speech runtime accuracy or AST one-to-one reachability.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-parity-speech-selector-check.json`
- **audio.shot_selector_coverage**: Shot header keys matching original unpatched MIPS PLAY lookup
  - Acceptance: Exact integer PLAY selector equality.
  - Limits: Same original-MIPS verification includes 773 shot keys. Request acceptance, global RNG, queue timing and audible behavior are not measured.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-parity-speech-selector-check.json`
- **audio.commentary_duck_runtime**: Original attract-match commentary lines whose start fires loop 0x24 with 38/40/25 and ducks the path volume 98 to 63, the ported values
  - Acceptance: Exact integer equality of the three percents and the path volume.
  - Limits: A live boot of the original read through PINE (2026-10-18); the rewrite's values are its constants and the tests that check its systems produce them, not a paired run.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-original-speech-attract-1-summary.json`
- **audio.speech_counter_seed_runtime**: Original speech manager seeds equal to the ported counter 18: the live boot's counter and three save states' middleware generators walked back
  - Acceptance: Exact integer equality.
  - Limits: The live boot's (summary) and the save states' (read offline; the walk is in docs/TRACE.md). A real console's boot timing may give another timer count and so another counter.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-original-speech-attract-1-summary.json`
- **audio.speech_queue_clock_runtime**: Original queued speech requests stamped with the 100 Hz timer (within the sampling's one count)
  - Acceptance: Within one hundredth of the timer at the first sample.
  - Limits: The queue slots read every frame through PINE; a request served within the frame it was posted is not seen.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-original-speech-attract-1-summary.json`
- **gameplay.body_scale_capture**: Production database body-scale function versus original captured normal-league scales
  - Acceptance: abs component error <1e-6; max XYZ=[1.1920929e-7,2.3841858e-7,2.3841858e-7]
  - Limits: Targeted native_body_scale_matches_original_gait_capture test rerun2026-10-13. All 198 are normal league; zero uniform/created. Repeated samples, not198 players. Evaluates production function; does not compare live rendered limbs.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-state9-active.json`
- **gameplay.script69_disc_target**: Production QFast decoding plus test-only native world bridge versus one original callback target
  - Acceptance: Euclidean target error <1e-4
  - Limits: Targeted native_script69_frame21_reconstructs_observed_callback_target test rerun2026-10-13. Production Bank::native_rotations is exercised, but world bridge is test-only and live World self-toss is absent. Mode 1 only.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-full-rig-active.json`
- **gameplay.script69_observed_target**: Offline bridge using observed frame 21 rig data versus original callback target
  - Acceptance: Euclidean error 2.0337667734806204e-5 (<1e-4)
  - Limits: Observed quaternion inputs, not production decoder/World. Non-atomic samples782/783/806; one event; no mirrored/bounce/planner coverage.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-sol-script69-target.json`
- **gameplay.native_flight_oracle**: Offline free-flight endpoint arithmetic versus original flight packets
  - Acceptance: Euclidean error <=1e-4; maximum 4.511455581220219e-5
  - Limits: Count independently derived from rows.flight and max bound. Offline double arithmetic, non-atomic capture; not production Ball::integrate and not evidence of script 69 execution.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-internal-flight-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.main_rig_positions**: Offline original-pose main-hierarchy position reconstruction
  - Acceptance: position error <=1e-4; max 0.4247388063812862
  - Limits: All 95 nonmatches retained. Bones0..20 only; player-shape branch assumed; head 21 and auxiliary22..42 excluded. Offline kernel is not live production pose_skeletons.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-sol-main-rig-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.main_rig_quaternions**: Offline original-pose main-hierarchy quaternion reconstruction
  - Acceptance: quaternion sign-equivalent error <=1e-4; max 0.0846916983783622
  - Limits: All 98 nonmatches retained. Same excluded bones/shape assumption/non-atomic capture as positions; denominator is repeated bone observations, not gameplay cases.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-sol-main-rig-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.gait_actor_step**: Offline native gait frame-step formula versus original actor+32c
  - Acceptance: f32 bit-exact; max error 0
  - Limits: Subset requires matching positive-root-distance record and no channel blend200000/flag 10. Capture contains 240 samples/1440 actor packets. Formula is not production gait selection or movement.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-state9-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.gait_channel_step**: Offline native gait frame-step formula versus original channel+3c
  - Acceptance: f32 bit-exact; max error 0
  - Limits: Same selected subset as actor-step; not an independent50 gameplay scenarios. Resolver category/rating/family/RNG order excluded.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-state9-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.gait_held_candidate**: Offline candidate-record held flag versus original actor held pointer
  - Acceptance: exact held/nonheld bit agreement
  - Limits: All 17 nonmatches retained; candidate matching is not full resolver eligibility or production comparison. Non-atomic packets may mix state; no cause assigned.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-state9-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.gait_physical_bound**: Offline native trig/physical-motion formula versus original actor motion
  - Acceptance: XZ Euclidean error <1e-5; max 0.41796819808898206
  - Limits: All 108 outside-bound observations retained; action/friction/contact/cross-tick causes not proven. Offline formula does not execute production Player::walk.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-physical-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.gait_physical_exact**: Bit-exact subset of the same offline native physical-motion comparison
  - Acceptance: zero recorded XZ error
  - Limits: Subset of567 in-bound observations, not additional independent cases; same non-atomic/action limits.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-native-gait-physical-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.fractional_display_oracle**: Offline QFast component interpolation/display normalization versus original local rotations
  - Acceptance: max component error <=1e-4; overall display max 0.8437739574716587
  - Limits: All 3688 nonmatches retained. Includes116 mirrored samples. Excludes root0, blends, non-QFast and guard failures; head 21 downstream writes and secondary channels uncaptured. Offline kernel, not live production Clip::rotation/pose_skeletons.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-pose-review/fractional-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.production_flight_pairs_numerical**: Compiled production free-flight core against captured original transitions
  - Acceptance: Every position xyz and native-per-update velocity xyz absolute error <=1e-4. Maximum position error 7.62939453125e-6; maximum native velocity error 2.980232238769531e-8.
  - Limits: 1998 adjacent pairs accounted for: 223 compared, 1775 source/domain exclusions retained. One/two updates inferred from timer delta; native bypass/progress flags uncaptured and reads non-atomic. Does not wire self-toss or prove ordinary-shot/World/contact parity. Historical legacy-only report; fresh counter-checked observations are reported separately.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-production-ball-flight-pairs.json`
- **gameplay.production_flight_pairs_exact**: Bit-exact compiled production free-flight comparisons after declared unit conversion
  - Acceptance: Exact float32 bits after native per-update velocity *60 into Rust and /60 back.
  - Limits: Same observations as numerical metric, not additional independent trials. Unit conversion and host/native arithmetic can lose exact equality while satisfying the numerical bound. Historical legacy-only report; fresh counter-checked observations are reported separately.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-production-ball-flight-pairs.json`
- **gameplay.production_flight_clock_numerical**: Compiled production free-flight, native counter-checked numerical agreement
  - Acceptance: Each xyz position and native velocity component absolute error <=1e-4; maxima 7.62939453125e-6 and 2.9802322387695312e-8.
  - Limits: 999 adjacent pairs:52 compared,947 excluded.1000 clock packets,989 bracket-matching,11 unstable. No residual filtering. Progress saturates; high-bit signed counters excluded. Stable bookends cannot prove atomic reads or interior continuity. Separate from223 legacy observations.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-production-ball-flight-clock-pairs.json`
- **gameplay.production_flight_clock_exact**: Compiled production free-flight, native counter-checked exact agreement
  - Acceptance: All six float32 components bit-equal after declared per-update/per-second conversion.
  - Limits: 999 adjacent pairs:52 compared,947 excluded.1000 clock packets,989 bracket-matching,11 unstable. No residual filtering. Progress saturates; high-bit signed counters excluded. Stable bookends cannot prove atomic reads or interior continuity. Separate from223 legacy observations.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-production-ball-flight-clock-pairs.json`
- **gameplay.source_only_staged_phase**: Historical source-only staged channel phase
  - Acceptance: Exact float32 bits; no residual-dependent admission.
  - Limits: Historical compiled source-only helper result before mutable-clock integration. All 4299 exclusions and 637 phase/187 rate differences retained. Covers one of sixteen plans, fourteen actor records, 1229 unique actor/source/ticks. Stable unpaused bookends do not prove completed scheduler updates. Not full pose, held-ball, replay or whole-game accuracy.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-staged-display-native\triggered-clock-comparison.json`
- **gameplay.source_only_staged_step**: Historical source-only staged configured rate
  - Acceptance: Exact float32 bits; no residual-dependent admission.
  - Limits: Historical compiled source-only helper result before mutable-clock integration. All 4299 exclusions and 637 phase/187 rate differences retained. Covers one of sixteen plans, fourteen actor records, 1229 unique actor/source/ticks. Stable unpaused bookends do not prove completed scheduler updates. - **presentation.camera_basis_kernel**: Native forward/right/up basis reconstruction (bounded oracle)
  - Acceptance: Maximum absolute component error 1.261133673e-7 (<1.27e-7).
  - Limits: 417 row-active +49 positioned +44 blend samples. Staged helper roll/transform channels excluded. Live reads are unpaused/non-atomic. This checks the oracle basis, not the production camera.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-camera-input-adapter-checks.json`
- **presentation.positioned_lens_target_kernel**: Positioned-helper geometric lens target (bounded oracle)
  - Acceptance: Maximum absolute scalar error 2.980232239e-8 (<2.99e-8).
  - Limits: Report lens_target. Active positioned helper 0x498950 differs from the row helper. Kernel agreement does not wire that controller into production. Non-atomic reads and emulator fixes apply.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-internal-reviewed-comparison.json`
- **presentation.positioned_lens_easing_kernel**: Positioned-helper easing changes (bounded float32 oracle)
  - Acceptance: Bit-exact match criterion; nine nonmatches retained, maximum absolute error 0.0004191100597.
  - Limits: Report lens_easing and rows. Skipped/mixed ticks remain possible; all nine differences remain eligible rather than being discarded. This is arithmetic kernel evidence, not row-camera or full production camera parity.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-internal-reviewed-comparison.json`
- **presentation.positioned_lens_repeat_kernel**: Independent positioned-lens repeat recurrence (bounded oracle)
  - Acceptance: Float32 predicted scalar is bit-exact for all six; seven geometric targets differ by at most 2.980232239e-8.
  - Limits: Separate small read-only capture: seven unique samples can skip ticks. Do not pool with the 88/97 change denominator or interpret as production controller coverage.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-camera-positioned-lens.json`
- **presentation.small_fov_angle_kernel**: Small-FOV native emitted angle (one restored-state oracle snapshot)
  - Acceptance: Bit-exact float value 10.043790817260742 degrees; observed absolute error zero.
  - Limits: One PINE batch after original portable state9 restore. Scalar 0.06590481847524643. The comparison used an oracle, not an invocation of production mode_vertical_fov with captured input, and does not prove complete projection/camera behavior.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-camera-native-projection-restored9.json`
- **presentation.small_fov_matrix_kernel**: Small-FOV native projection coefficients (same oracle snapshot)
  - Acceptance: Bit-exact criterion fails for both m00/m11; each absolute error is 9.5367431640625e-7 (one ULP at these values).
  - Limits: Both finite differences are retained. Host tangent/reciprocal rounding accounts for the documented numerical bound; no complete production matrix, crop, depth, roll or GS comparison was performed.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-camera-native-projection-restored9.json`
- **presentation.production_projection_angle**: Actual production projection angle against original captured scalar
  - Acceptance: Exact horizontal-degree float32 bits: 0 ULP.
  - Limits: One component observation, not any per-mode runtime capture. Supplied scalar generation, native controller, basis/pose, conditional offsets, depth and crop behavior are not compared. Assumes full viewport; native viewport scale fields and context widescreen branch are uncaptured. Bevy Y coefficient is explicitly negated to native convention. Historical pre-rounding-fix production measurement; current consumer results are separately reported.

## Cases and next probes

### Audio

- **audio.action_lookup** — matched / exact / static / active: Resolve each of the 3,196 action-header (voice, start) keys to its native PLAY selector.
  - Acceptance: Exact discrete decisions and values; no tolerance.
  - Limits: Static instruction execution proves the bounded selector construction only; it does not establish runtime request sequence or audible accuracy. Last re-pinned 2026-10-24 after the public-beta rename, names moved to LEGAL.md and neutral save tags, no behaviour change (re-pins that leave the cited region unchanged replace this line).
  - Next: Capture original and production selected PLAY slots for identical action requests.
  - Evidence: `docs/SPEECH_RECOVERY.md` — Reconstruction and checks; Original FUN_001af230 voice trees return these selector slots; start is big-endian u 16 times 0x100.
  - Evidence: `tools/verify_speech_lookup.py` — main / Executable.lookup; Re-executed 2026-10-15 against the user's unpatched USA ISO: Verified 3969 PLAY selectors across 44 voice/type headers against original MIPS instructions. This includes all 3,196 action keys.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-audio/src/speech.rs` — PlayerSpeech::load; Re-read 2026-10-15: PlayerSpeech::load reads each action header line's big-endian start and resolves it with speech_lookup::lookup(voice, start), the table the tool checks against FUN_001af230 with the key start*0x100 (FUN_001af1e8 passes packet word 1 unchanged).
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-parity-speech-selector-check.json` — action / catalog_comparisons; Actual original-MIPS and production selector values retained for every header key,together with command stdout,coverage counts and executable/header/source SHA-256 digests.
- **audio.shot_lookup** — matched / exact / static / active: Resolve all 773 shot-header keys through FUN_001c4b20.
  - Acceptance: Exact discrete decisions and values; no tolerance.
  - Limits: Catalog lookup only; no whole-match speech timing proof. Last re-pinned 2026-10-24 after the public-beta rename, names moved to LEGAL.md and neutral save tags, no behaviour change (re-pins that leave the cited region unchanged replace this line).
  - Next: Capture original launch callback selectors and production launch selectors.
  - Evidence: `docs/SPEECH_RECOVERY.md` — Candidate selection and remaining limits; Shot lookup uses the distinct native tree for all 773 lines.
  - Evidence: `tools/verify_speech_lookup.py` — main / Executable.lookup; The same original-MIPS verification, re-executed 2026-10-15, checks shot keys at entry 0x1c4b20.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-audio/src/speech.rs` — PlayerSpeech::load; Re-read 2026-10-15: PlayerSpeech::load sends type-0x2003 headers to speech_lookup::lookup_shot, the FUN_001c4b20 table (FUN_001af1e8 takes it for any header type other than 0x2002).
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/street-parity-speech-selector-check.json` — shot / catalog_comparisons; Actual original-MIPS and production selector values retained for every header key,together with command stdout,coverage counts and executable/header/source SHA-256 digests.
- **audio.play_indirection** — matched / structural / static / active: Resolve player selectors through the ABK PLAY list, including aliases 2173/2174.

Last re-pinned 2026-10-24 after the public-beta rename, names moved to LEGAL.md and neutral save tags, no behaviour change (re-pins that leave the cited region unchanged replace this line).

  - Evidence: `crates/street-audio/tests/audio_disc.rs` — the_player_lines_find_their_streams; Disc test follows node 27's 3, 971-entry list and checks ABK/AST offsets; this corroborates the recovered native indirection.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-audio/src/aems.rs` — play / sound; Production clamps input 6 into the node's sound list, then masks the returned sound ID with 0x3fff to resolve stream-table entries, following the original path.
- **audio.metadata_masks** — matched / structural / static / active: Apply action selectors [2, 4] with masks 0x7fff/7 and shot [2, 3] with 0x7fff/0x3f.
  - Acceptance: Exact discrete decisions and values; no tolerance.
  - Limits: Static recovery plus production tests. Categories 6 and 11..13 have no placed caller in the rewrite; slot 27's missed-shot requests (0x1a4550: category 14 shot mask 2, then category 4) have no traced rewrite sender. PLAYERS.EVT descriptors 0x2002 [2,4] masks [0x7fff,7] and 0x2003 [2,3] [0x7fff,0x3f]. Caller corrections: categories 9/10 belong to the block/steal handlers (slots 16/15), 0/1 to the rebound pickup (slot 17); a pass (slot 9, 0x142ff8) asks nothing.

  - Evidence: `crates/street-audio/tests/audio_disc.rs` — native_player_event_rules_select_action_and_launch_lines_on_disc; Disc test reads PLAYERS.EVT bytes and selects the matching action and shot lines for every voice.
- **audio.player_branch_draws** — matched / structural / static / active: Choose action/shot branches without replacement and consume all order and branch-chance draws.
  - Acceptance: Exact discrete decisions and values; no tolerance.
  - Limits: Static recovery and production-path equality with an independent draw sequence.   - Acceptance: Exact button edges/action tick; exact gait family hysteresis thresholds 40/59, 39/61, 41/60; exact D-pad words and strengths (64, 90 on a diagonal) less the camera's yaw word; quarter-wave 1024 cosine reflection.

The notes that follow state the current limits. Still the rewrite's: the held ball the swipe reads is the holder's posed hand on his gait clip (the dribble's flight, FUN_0014e060's 0x10/0x20 frames, not ported; the simulation's own ball still rides Ball::carry); actors pose their gait or swipe clip only (no channel blends, no trick, pass, shot or jump clips); online matches carried no animations and played the stand-ins (until 2026-10-20); blocks are still the rewrite's swat at the release (the original's FUN_0015ce50/FUN_0016d888 test the posed hands in flight and knock the ball loose at 0.35 an update and 0.125 up; no shot is caught in either attract capture). Without the disc's animations the swipe keeps the stand-in (tested once at update 13). 2026-10-16 (the session's generator): docs/TRACE.md "The session's generator". The original seeds its shared generator once at the boot from the clock (FUN_002f5578: the RTC's BCD second, minute and hour, s + m*60 + h*3600 + 1), and FUN_001dbe30 reseeds it from the 100 Hz timer count 0x4b32e4 whenever FUN_001dbb20 sets a team for a side no controller holds (the attract match's picks FUN_001db7f8, Play It's defaults FUN_001db968, Choose Team's commit FUN_0026fd00, a story event's opponent FUN_001e91a0); nothing in a match or a rematch reseeds it. Every captured state walks back to such a reseed: save states 1 and 2, the three played captures and the eight rematches of that boot all to 18,061 (180.61 s after the boot; the save states hold the count past it and the rate 100), the fresh boot's Pick Up game to 13,943, 48 draws before its update 0, the first attract match to 20,445, 46 before its update 0 and 48 before update 1. So the game's pair is the 47th and 48th draws after the last reseed in both first games captured, whose front ends differ (the 46 belong to the loading and construction, untraced). Built: World::from_generator makes CONSTRUCTION_DRAWS (46, the rewrite's own count from those two) and then FUN_0012e798's pair for a points game (every game but trick points and Gamebreaker Wins, as FUN_002f3000 builds them); street-game's Session (session.rs) seeds the generator from the clock (UTC+9, BCD as the boot does; --seed and scripted runs pin it), reseeds it from the hundredths since it began at Choose Team's commit and the story starts for a side no person holds, builds each match on it (load_match) and takes the match's generator back when the match is left, so a rematch draws from where the game ended. The fresh boot's reseed rebuilds its captured pair [2, 17] and its update 0's six words; the seven update-0 captures rebuild theirs from the state 48 draws earlier. Narrowed, not closed: World::new keeps the labelled [2, 16] for bare-seed worlds (tests, tools, a dedicated server's default match); the 46 draws' uses and any per-court count (the second attract capture's opening is not readable), the front end's own draws from the shared generator (Choose Court's Random FUN_002688f0, Random League Team FUN_0026ef88, the attract picks) and the match's own draw count (8.3 an update in the side-fade capture) are not reproduced, so the state a rematch is built on is not the original's. 2026-10-16 (the facing angle): timing::facing_angle, the angle FUN_0016b4b8 branches on (FUN_0018d290: the facing word less the direction from the rim, 0 facing it), measured the sim's heading on its mirrored court and came to 512 less the original's; it now takes the original's word (the units of PI - heading) and direction (atan2(rim x - x, z - rim z)) and equals the original's angle on all 93,654 actor updates of the side-fade capture (docs/TRACE.md "The chooser's facing angle in the original's frame"). On the update before each of the 23 side presses the chooser picks the decided left_r 2 and right_l 8 (through World::step as well, where the replay now carries the shooter's facing, motion and hand) and the drawn branch's fade or a side hook on the other 13; the facing-away (400..600) and runner branches read the rim's side and its back the right way round. The sim's computer players' matches run on another draw sequence (attract capture 1: 145 picks, the runner 16, side fades 12, against 124, 16 and 18 before); every attract-capture test passes. Still the rewrite's own: state bit 11 (jumpshot_2_fadeaway) read clear, the sim's heading a float where the original turns a word. Lead: timing::sector (FUN_0018d768) measures the same way and may read the original's sector s as 4 - s; not checked. The points game's stage bounds are drawn every game: ten games (the save state, eight rematches, a Pick Up game after a fresh boot) hold nine pairs, every lower bound 0..5 and upper 15..17, and where captured from update 0 the pair is the shared generator's last two outputs (the sim's Rng stepped back twice draws it and lands on the captured state). World::stage_bounds stays at the rewrite's own 2 and 16, a known difference: a draw from the sim's per-match generator would be 5 and 16 for every small seed; matching the original needs the match's generator to carry the session's state. New lead, not changed: the sim's timing::facing_angle comes to 512 minus the original's angle on the mirrored court (6 of the 23 side presses fall outside their band; the facing-away and runner branches read the rim's side and its back the wrong way round) (closed 2026-10-16, above). The catch-up stage is the larger scaled score: FUN_0012eac0 forms it with pmaxw at 0x12eb6c, and the decompile's OR is the project's Ghidra copy, which rewrites MMI instructions as or (tools/ee_patch.py); FUN_0018c788 and FUN_00145b48 take the larger with slt/movn, and Gamebreaker Wins adds 100 for a side with a Level 1 gamebreaker scored (now ported). The side fades jump through game callback 18 (FUN_00172788: 15 / fps up, the landing by FUN_0019bed8 as 9f stores it), so their gates open by themselves at 45 (right_r, left_l) and 50 (right_l, left_r), tabled and walked from the executable; the sim's gate now opens by itself after a wait (it compared the stalled script tick with latest and so waited for the button alone). On the captures: 45 presses on the gate rule, 44 replays through World::step with the five side fades, 22 makes of 27 against 20.87 +/- 1.75 expected through catch_up_cell with the captured bounds (20.95 with the OR, 12.64 +/- 2.27 without the person's bonuses). Still the rewrite's own: the points game's stage bounds start at 2 and 16 (FUN_0012e798 draws rand() % 6 and 15 + rand() % 3 from a generator the front end has run on; the captured match drew 4 and 15; drawn first from the sim's generator they would be 5 and 16 for every seed), the side fades' sideways drift (traced, not in the sim's jump curve), and no capture holds a side fade past its cap. 2026-10-15 (the person's timing): two pad-bearing captures of a played match (docs/TRACE.md "The person's shots, passes and catches") showed the release gate the walkers had counted as nothing: opcode 0x86 re-runs callback 19 (FUN_00174c10) every update and a person's jump shot waits there while the button is held, until the actor is 15 updates from landing (5 for the clips outside 0x3d9218), the ball leaving release - gate later; the computer passes at once. The sim's shot, dunk and lay-up ticks were an update early (the press update is the script's tick 0 now); Action::Shooting carries the gate, its self-opening tick and the stall; the heave is chosen from the back court as FUN_0016b4b8 does; the fadeaway only on scripts with callback 17. The make odds' inputs with the person's bonuses are consistent with the captured makes (22 of 26 against 20.9 expected; the plain odds expect 12.7); the roll itself cannot be pinned to a draw (the computer players draw 7..19 numbers an update before the callback). - **gameplay.pass_interception_catch** — mismatched / structural / runtime / active: Match direct/kick/special/alley-oop pass timing, target choice, trajectory, interceptions and catches.
  - Acceptance: Same native target/kind, launch/catch/interception tick and recipient; trajectory <=1e-4.
  - Limits: Notes dated 2026-10-16 to 2026-10-16 (how this case was built and re-pinned) were moved out on 2026-10-26 to keep the scorecard under the publish check's size limit; `git log -p docs/parity/gameplay.json` keeps them. The notes that follow state the current limits. Still the rewrite's: the actors' poses are their gait clips (no channel blends), the deflector's cooldown (+0x1cc) and later touches, the defenders' 35% bar, the receiver excluded rather than swept, online matches played the stand-in (until 2026-10-20). 2026-10-16 (pass flights): the flight is now the original's: FUN_001611d8 times a pass max(distance / 0.75196, 20) updates, truncated, on the game's passer-to-receiver distance when it leads him or the passer-to-spot distance when it does not (the aim from FUN_0015f930 at the press, the re-evaluation at the release), 40 for the first pass after the actors' reset, and FUN_0014c0c0's velocity lands the ball on the point in exactly that many updates (gameplay.pass_flights: 369 of 386 captured ordinary flights exact, the rest within 5). Still the rewrite's own: the point's height (the catch row's posed hands in the original), the catch (FUN_00137db8 sweeps the ball's last move against the receiver's bone spheres and takes it 0..5 updates before it arrives; the rewrite takes it as it arrives, which is the original's commonest case, 66 of 155), the opponents' first-35% bar (FUN_00173570), the spot of an airborne receiver and of "fast break" and "post cut", the lob to a leaper (FUN_00162aa8 needs the leap script's span) and the trick passes' legs (FUN_001488e8). 2026-10-16 (held side fades): a third played capture keeps the catch rule on 117 receptions; one catch begun only 3 updates before the ball arrived held 15 where the rule gives 10 (docs/TRACE.md "Held side fades and the stage bounds across games", A late catch): the sim does not model a late start. 2026-10-15 (the person's timing): a person's passes release at their rows' callback 38 like the computer's, and L3 draws the rated kick rows (pass_sidekick 26); a person's receivers hold the catch like the computer's, but one running reception cleared bit 9 at the flight's end (the catch_run scripts' own 9c), so the running receivers' 9 is one of two kinds. Release timing (2026-10-15): the ball stays in the passer's hands from the press to the drawn row script's callback 38, the row from a port of FUN_0015f930's filter and draw (sector, distance band, standing/running, hand, kick, phase 6, alley-oop, heave, handoff); the catch holds the receiver for the flight's nominal end plus the catch row's tail (FUN_001611d8). Both captures agree on every row the channel names. Not carried: the legends' rows, the receiver's spot 25 updates ahead, landing-within-10, the running row's velocity, the per-record rating 7 (Handles stands in), the catch chooser's bit-11 and 0x3d65b8/0x40000 gates, the running catch's 9 (measured, its script not identified); the trajectory, interception reach and the trick passes remain the rewrite's own. Earlier: Release timing (2026-10-15): the original's ball stays in the passer's hands from the press (ball state 4) to the pass script's callback 38; inbounds (row 10) release after 18 updates in both captures and in the rewrite; live passes spread 10..32 by row in the original, and the rewrite releases all of them after its own 20 (the row filter FUN_0015f930 0x160870..0x160ebc is not ported); tips, trick passes and lobs to a leaper are thrown at once. Independent Gemini review 2026-10-14 (G08,G05): the four synthesized value sets are not the original 93-row 28-byte table; the trajectory, catch and interception rules remain the rewrite's own. 2026-10-15 (later): the rewrite's interception rule (any defender within 1.8, 25% + 45% of Steals once) is replaced by the original's last-quarter roll and the counter stance's catch; interceptions fell from 16 a match to 0..1 (the original's 3 of 131 live passes, two by counter stances). - **gameplay.game_phase_machine** — matched / numerical / runtime / active: Keep the game's phase (game+0xf4), possession (game+0x110) and game+0x4a4 as the original's game object does: the ball's moments posted to the game listener at once (slot 24 for every give, slot 11 for every launch, the basket's and the shot clock's restarts) and the game's update after the ball (FUN_0013d868: the end of the game, FUN_001405f0, FUN_0013e4c8, FUN_0013ee10), and drive the computer players and the restarts from it.
  - Acceptance: Per capture, the machine's phase equals the captured game+0xf4 in at least 99.9% of compared updates and never differs for two updates in a row; its possession equals game+0x110 in every update; game+0x4a4 agrees in at least 99.9%. Measured: 40,873 of 40,886, 14,149 of 14,155 and 30,909 of 30,919 updates, possession in every one, game+0x4a4 40,883, 14,154 and 30,917; every miss one update (18 inbound releases, 9 held balls or passes within a unit of half court, a rebound just below 6, a launch read mid-update).
  - Limits: Runtime, the machine fed with the captures' ball events: what the rewrite's own ball and players feed it is compared only through the turnovers and phase counts of its computer games (every turnover out of phase 5 to phase 4, as the original's six; time in phase 4 and back-court passing not matched, the AI's bring-up and pass flights). Fed from the capture: new holders (the tip's take and pass inside one update from the ball's previous holder), launches, the six ready flags (a restart when any falls), the hold game+0x104 and the tip's start. Stood in for, not captured: the script manager's state (the staged gamebreaker's camera, the one camera seen only in phases 5 and 6), the score (a match reaching phase 9 is won by its last basket) and a basket's moment (the ball down through a ring). The rewrite's own: World::sync_game_phase for a World phase set from outside the transitions; its timed dead ball and formation restart end phase 6 when they hand over the ball; the script manager's states are a staged sequence; the won test is score_basket's goal check; the settled test reads the holder's shot, dunk and lay-up actions. - **gameplay.restart_pickup_and_loose_ball** — matched / structural / static / active: In a restart the restarting side's tail takes the loose ball as in play (0x15c3c8: FUN_0016cd28's reach, the scoop) and the other side's update leaves it (FUN_0015da28: not in phase 6); "loose ball" (FUN_0010e0a0) runs at the mode's speed level (2 while barred), meets a ball above his height plus 2 where it comes down or jumps for it, runs at a lower one, dives in the ball's bounds, and in a restart waits out his bar and cooldown at the ball.
  - Acceptance: The same rule.
  - Limits: Without a replay the original's inbounder is not driven for about 20 updates (speed level 0), so its take comes at 41..44 against the rewrite's about 30, those restarts 111..120 at the shortest against 130..135. - **presentation.online_instant_replays** — matched / structural / runtime / active: Online matches play the game's replays as a match alone does: a basket's instant replay (the director's roll on the shared generator; the match plays its creator's Replays option), the won game's forced replay, the gamebreaker task's two slowed replays, a person's replay and cancel presses. The original's replay manager holds the gameplay while a replay plays (FUN_0033d380(0x401b2) suspends the gameplay task groups), so the manager, director and task are simulated (street_sim::replay, their control alone) and the online Match steps them on every machine after the world: while a replay plays the world isn't stepped and its clock stands still while the match's tick, the network's, runs on; the replays and the match's tick are in the match's snapshot and checksum; each machine shows its own drawing of the updates replayed (tape.rs); a finished match stays up from the end of its won game's replay.
  - Acceptance: The same replays at the same match ticks on every machine; no confirmed state differing; the game's replay tests passing unchanged with the control moved.
  - Limits: The frames a replay shows are each machine's own drawing of the updates: a remote pad that arrived late can leave a tick or two of a guess among them (the match itself is never affected), and where a machine stepped two ticks in one frame a tick shows the frame before. The staged Level 2 gamebreaker's rerun is presentation.online_staged_rerun. The replays' behaviour against the original is presentation.effects_and_replay_runtime's. 2026-10-20 (the online pass's last gaps): the staged Level 2's rerun is a replay too, mode 7 (presentation.online_staged_rerun); the match holds the world through it and through its script's own end update (Replays::holds_world); the determinism test counts the staged reruns at the referee's ticks on every machine. 2026-10-23: the match's snapshot and checksum carry the burst hold too (protocol 23). Last re-pinned 2026-10-26 after the distance tables' sort (re-pins that leave the cited region unchanged replace this line).

- **presentation.online_staged_rerun** — matched / structural / runtime / active: A Level 2 gamebreaker's staged script ends with its replay command's slowed rerun (FUN_003355c8 dispatches category 1 opcode 0 at its frame plus its span, the script's last frame, before its stop): FUN_00334b30 puts the script manager in state 5 (FUN_00336188: its replay frame and clock at the span's first frame, the command's percent) and asks the replay manager for the span in mode 7 (FUN_00328fc0(1.0, manager, span, 0, 7), the speed gated out by the running script, then FUN_00329ac8(percent / 100)); the script manager steps its cameras through the span while the manager plays (FUN_00336868); the replay holds the game like any; its end (mode 7: FUN_00142648, FUN_003362a0) hands the script back, whose stop ends it in an update of its own, no restart snapped; no press skips it (FUN_00130b88: kind 1 scripts); a freeze whose word 12 is 1 holds the replay manager too (FUN_00336340's 0x1382). Every match plays it: alone, online (every machine alike, the network's tick running on) and in a lesson.
  - Acceptance: The School's run bit for bit (the manager's shown count, speed and frames left, the script's replay frame) and within 0.05 s on the wall clock; every machine starting the rerun at the same match tick, no checksum differing.
  - Limits: No played match of the original's has had its staged rerun grabbed: the School's lesson run is the runtime evidence (its frames were not grabbed), the cameras are the scripts' own records at the script manager's replay frame. Only one of the sixteen plans has run in the original (GamLR2); the others' commands are read from the disc. The replay command's count (word 12 + 1) is one on all sixteen; more is not modelled. 2026-10-21 (item 7): the rerun's camera takes the staged helper's lens and roll, measured on the School's Level 2 rerun (presentation.staged_camera_lens_roll_follow). 2026-10-23: the match also holds a staged Level 2's 31 updates after its press for GB2's burst (Match::burst_hold, street_net::game::LEVEL2_BURST_HOLD, checked against the banner's 32 updates), as the game played alone holds them; the disc test asserts it holds. - **presentation.staged_camera_lens_roll_follow** — matched / numerical / runtime / active: A staged script's camera (plays, freezes, reruns, court intros) is the staged helper's: the node (FUN_00331d08) puts tan(fov / 2) in +0x98, read through the shared projection (zoom 1.3), the roll (heading - 180) at +0x1b0 the renderer turns the view by, and a following record aims at its script actor or the ball, each on its state's clock: play at the frame before the manager's, the freeze's camera from its own frame, the replay's at the float clock before its step.
  - Acceptance: The script cameras' fields bit for bit (a torn read's fields this update's or the next's); eye and target within 0.1 of the node for following cameras; the lens 1e-6; the view columns 2e-4.
  - Limits: Measured on one plan (GamLR2): its play, freeze and replay cameras bit for bit in four probes; the other fifteen plans rest on the same code. A following camera's point is the drawn player's root and bone. Not modelled: the nodes' state between updates (a replay faster than one frame an update could step over a key's frame and hold that key short of its end; every replay on the disc runs at 25 to 50 percent, so none does), and a key whose previous record is a camera that keeps the last position reads the record's position.   - Evidence: `crates/street-game/src/banner.rs` — burst; burst_wiping; Banner::draw (GB2's burst); tests gb2s_burst_wipes_one_wedge_every_four_updates, a_staged_level_2_waits_for_the_bursts_first_wedge_to_go; The burst and the wipe the press waits for.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-game/src/screen_fx.rs` — ScreenFx (hear, stage, draw); Quad::draw; tests; The overlay's tint, flash and blur and the staged effect players.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-render/src/screen_fx.rs` — ScreenLayers; ScreenCopy; copy_pass; The passes, before and after the UI.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-game/src/game.rs` — step_sim (the burst's hold); The press's hold.
  - Evidence: `crates/street-assets/src/timeline.rs` — Timeline::effect_layers; Effect::field; The effect players' records and keys.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/claude-l2look-20261023/SLUS-20651_20261023001826.gs` — the frame's full-screen passes (draws 6088, 6089, 8112..8114); The original's copy (alpha 0x30, colour 0xa0) and its quads, in a staged frame after the freeze.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/claude-l2look-20261023/l2orig/fam01/fam01-013-28532.png` — whole frame; The original's burst wiping over the graded court.
  - Evidence: `$EVIDENCE/claude-l2look-20261023/ours-fx3/f-064.png` — whole frame; The rewrite's, at the same plan and region.

## How this becomes an accuracy assessment

1. Complete and review the scenario inventory against the original's subsystems, modes,
   input boundaries and state transitions before calling it whole-game coverage.
2. Run paired original/rewrite scenarios with the same initial state and input schedule.
   Record exact equality separately from numerical agreement and explain every exclusion.
3. Preserve original captures, rewrite outputs, tolerances and reproducible comparison commands.
   Compare production code paths, not just disconnected arithmetic kernels.
4. Publish per-area agreement and untested coverage. Fix any global weighting in advance;
   never let thousands of easy asset/lookup checks outweigh unresolved gameplay behavior.
